// @public (undocumented)
type TableGlideCustomProps<R extends ObjectForExtending_3, SR = unknown> = {
    columns: ColumnGlideLast<R, SR>[];
    rows: readonly R[];
    bottomSummaryRows?: readonly SR[];
    rowSize?: SIZE_2;
    rowHeight?: RowHeightProp<R>;
    maxAutoRowHeight?: number;
    ctxs?: ObjectForExtending_3;
    renderGroupHeader?: (args: RenderGroupHeaderArgs) => 'default' | {
        defaultWithCustomGroupName: string;
    } | CanvasEl;
    groupAlignMap?: Map<string, SpanAlignment>;
    refTable?: Ref<DataEditorRef>;
    onColumnsReorder?: (sourceKey: string, targetKey: string) => void;
    portalEventTargetRef?: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>;
    unstickyHeader?: boolean;
    resizableColumn?: boolean;
    containerRef?: Ref<HTMLDivElement>;
    containerClassName?: string;
    containerStyle?: CSSProperties;
    renderOverlayFeatures?: (ctx: {
        containerElement: HTMLDivElement;
        renderInContainer: (children: ReactNode) => ReactPortal | null;
    }) => ReactNode;
    hideGrid?: boolean;
    contentStateNode?: ReactNode;
    containerSlot?: ReactNode;
    fullScreened?: boolean;
    editorOverlayPortal?: 'inside';
    groupHeaderHeight?: number[];
    highlightActiveType?: HighlightActiveType_2;
    cellsSelectionMode?: CellsSelectionMode;
    focusOnStateChange?: boolean;
    onSelectionEmit?: (selection: GridSelection) => void;
    onSelectionSync?: (selection: GridSelection) => void;
    onColumnSelectionChange?: (selectedColumnKeys: string[]) => void;
    pendingColumnSelectionKeys?: string[] | null;
    onColumnSelectionApplied?: () => void;
    highlightActiveRow?: number;
    onHighlightActiveRowChange?: (row: number | undefined, rowData?: R | undefined) => void;
    highlightActiveRowControlled?: boolean;
    enableColumnSelection?: boolean;
    enableRowSelection?: boolean;
    enableSelectAll?: boolean;
    outlineEachRange?: boolean;
    checkboxSelectedRowIndexes?: ReadonlySet<number>;
    checkboxVisibleRowIndexes?: ReadonlySet<number>;
    hoverEffects?: HoverEffectsConfig;
    appearance?: AppearanceConfig;
    enableLowDprHairline?: boolean;
    isErrorRow?: (row: R) => boolean;
    isCellEdited?: (row: R, columnKey: string) => boolean;
    collectRowErrors?: boolean;
};

// @public (undocumented)
type TableGlideInstanceContextMenu<R extends ObjectForExtending_2, SR = unknown, CustomCtxs extends ObjectForExtending_2 = {}> = {
    onHeaderContextMenu?: (...args: [
    ...Parameters<NonNullable<TableGlideProps<R, SR>['onHeaderContextMenu']>>,
    TableInfoBase<R, SR, CustomCtxs>
    ]) => void;
    onCellContextMenu?: (...args: [
    ...Parameters<NonNullable<TableGlideProps<R, SR>['onCellContextMenu']>>,
    TableInfoWithRow<R, SR, CustomCtxs>
    ]) => void;
};

// @public (undocumented)
type TableGlideInstanceProps<R extends ObjectForExtending_2, SR = unknown, CustomCtxs extends ObjectForExtending_2 = {}> = Omit<TableGlideProps<R, SR>, 'columns' | 'onHeaderContextMenu' | 'onCellContextMenu' | 'onCellClicked'> & {
    columns: readonly ColumnGlideInstance<R, SR, CustomCtxs>[];
    onHeaderContextMenuDropdown?: HeaderContextMenuDropdownProps<R, SR, CustomCtxs>;
    onCellContextMenuDropdown?: CellContextMenuDropdownProps<R, SR>;
    groupHeaderRowHeight?: number;
    containerProps?: {
        className?: string;
        style?: React.CSSProperties;
        ref?: React.Ref<HTMLDivElement>;
    };
    contentStateOverlay?: TableContentStateOverlay;
    renderOverlayFeatures?: (ctx: {
        containerElement: HTMLDivElement;
        renderInContainer: (children: React.ReactNode) => React.ReactPortal | null;
    }) => React.ReactNode;
    CellReadOnlyEditor: (props: CellReadOnlyEditorGlideInstanceProps<R, SR>) => ReactNode;
    onRowsChange?: (rows: R[], data: RowsChangeDataGlideInstance<R, SR, CustomCtxs>) => void;
    onCellClicked?: (cell: readonly [number, number], info: TableInfoWithRow<R, SR, CustomCtxs>, event: CellClickedEventArgs) => void;
    stickyColumnsConfig?: StickyColumnsConfig<ColumnGlideInstance<R, SR, CustomCtxs>>;
    stickyRowsConfig?: StickyRowsConfig<R>;
} & TableGlideInstanceContextMenu<R, SR, CustomCtxs>;

// @public (undocumented)
type TableGlideProps<R extends ObjectForExtending_3, SR> = Omit<GlideProps, keyof TableGlideCustomProps<R, SR> | 'getCellContent' | 'drawGroupHeader' | 'ref' | 'groupHeaderHeight'> & TableGlideCustomProps<R, SR>;

// @public (undocumented)
type TableInfoBase<R extends ObjectForExtending_2, SR = unknown, CustomCtxs extends ObjectForExtending_2 = {}> = {
    column: ColumnOrColumnGroupGlideInstance<R, SR, CustomCtxs>;
    ctxs: CustomCtxs;
    refTable?: React.Ref<DataEditorRef>;
};

// @public (undocumented)
type TableInfoWithRow<R extends ObjectForExtending_2, SR = unknown, CustomCtxs extends ObjectForExtending_2 = {}> = TableInfoBase<R, SR, CustomCtxs> & {
    row: R;
};

// @public (undocumented)
interface TableNotification {
    // (undocumented)
    code: TableNotificationCode;
    // (undocumented)
    level: TableNotificationLevel;
    // (undocumented)
    message: string;
    // (undocumented)
    meta?: Record<string, unknown>;
    // (undocumented)
    type: TableNotificationType;
}

// @public (undocumented)
type TableNotificationCode = 'multi-range-scattered' | 'readonly-abort' | 'overflow-abort' | 'validation-skipped' | 'no-selection';

// @public (undocumented)
type TableNotificationLevel = 'error' | 'warning' | 'info';

// @public (undocumented)
interface TableNotificationsConfig {
    // (undocumented)
    onNotification: (event: TableNotification) => void;
}

// @public (undocumented)
type TableNotificationType = 'copy' | 'paste' | 'fill' | 'pin';

// @public (undocumented)
export type TableProps<FilterStateType extends ObjectForExtending, RowIdType extends string | number, HeaderContextValueType extends ObjectForExtending, RowContextValueType extends ObjectForExtending, RowType extends ObjectForExtending, SummaryRowType = unknown> = {
    columnConfig: readonly ColumnConfig<RowType, SummaryRowType>[];
    rows: RowType[];
    topSummaryRows?: SummaryRowType[];
